Gele highlight achter koppen: laat hem op een woordgrens eindigen i.p.v. vaste 150px */ function fitHighlights(){document.querySelectorAll('.background-title').forEach(function (bg){var wrap = bg.parentElement; if (!wrap) return; var heading = wrap.querySelector('h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,.h3-right,[class*="title"]'); if (!heading || heading === bg) return; var walker = document.createTreeWalker(heading, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT); var node = walker.nextNode(); while (node && !node.textContent.trim()) node = walker.nextNode(); if (!node) return; var text = node.textContent; var start = text.search(/\S/); var range = document.createRange(); var hRect = heading.getBoundingClientRect(); var target = Math.min(160, hRect.width * 0.6); var re = /\S+/g, m, best = 0, firstTop = null; while ((m = re.exec(text))){range.setStart(node, start); range.setEnd(node, m.index + m[0].length); var rects = range.getClientRects(); var last = rects[rects.length - 1]; if (firstTop === null) firstTop = rects[0].top; if (last.top > firstTop + 2) break; /* niet over een regelafbreking heen */ var w = last.right - hRect.left; if (best === 0 || w <= target) best = w; else break;}if (!best) return; bg.style.right = 'auto'; bg.style.maxWidth = 'none'; bg.style.width = Math.round(best + 15) + 'px';});}window.addEventListener('load', fitHighlights); window.addEventListener('resize', function (){clearTimeout(fitHighlights._t); fitHighlights._t = setTimeout(fitHighlights, 150);}); if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(fitHighlights);})(); (function (){var mqMobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)'); var root = document.documentElement; /* Mega-menu: echte bovenkant doorgeven aan de CSS, zodat de max-hoogte klopt met het zichtbare scherm */ function updMegaTop(){var mm = document.querySelector('.navigation .mega-menu.w--open'); if (!mm) return; var top = Math.max(0, Math.round(mm.getBoundingClientRect().top)); root.style.setProperty('--mm-top', top + 'px');}/* 2. Lint (horizontale nav) op mobiel: fade aan de zijkant zodat duidelijk is dat je kunt scrollen. Twee losse overlays boven het lint, met dezelfde kleurverloop als de header. Omdat het lint zelf geen mask meer heeft, blijft de fade ook staan als het mega-menu open is. */ function parseRgb(s){var m = s && s.match(/rgba?\(([^)]+)\)/g); if (!m || m.length < 2) return null; return m.slice(0, 2).map(function (c){return c.replace(/rgba?\(|\)/g, '').split(',').map(function (v){return parseFloat(v);});});}function mix(a, b, t){return 'rgb(' + [0, 1, 2].map(function (i){return Math.round(a[i] + (b[i] - a[i]) * t);}).join(',') + ')';}function initLint(){document.querySelectorAll('.navigation .nav-links').forEach(function (el){if (el.__lint) return;                   <script>
  document.querySelectorAll(".language_modal").forEach((wrapper) => {
  const form = wrapper.querySelector("form");
  const input = wrapper.querySelector("input[name='language_code']");
  if (form && input) {
    form.querySelectorAll("a").forEach((link) => {
      link.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
        input.value = event.currentTarget.dataset.value;
        form.submit();
      });
    });
  }
});
</script>

              <a aria-label="Kratje" data-cart-target="true" href="/cart" class="circle-icon w-inline-block">
                <div li-element="mini-cart-item-count" class="cart-quantity" x-data="{ items: 0 }" x-text="items" @carttotalitems.window="items = event.detail">0</div><img loading="lazy" src="//debiersalon.nl/cdn/shop/t/117/assets/beercase.png?v=138010026655020353601790840590" alt="" class="icon">

              <script>
    document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {

    Alpine.data('handleMinicart', () => ({
        init() {
            console.log('handleMinicart init')
        },
        cart: {
            note: null,
            attributes: {},
            items: [],
            currency: window.Shopify.currency.active,
            cart_level_discount_applications: [],
            response: {
                result : {},
                show : false,
                timeout : 5000,
            },
            item_count: 0,
            total_price:0,
            total_weight: 0,
            total_discount: 0,
            original_total_price: 0,
            items_subtotal_price: 0
        },
        _abortController : null,
        initAbortController() {
            if(this._abortController) {
                this._abortController.abort('abort previous request');
            }
            this._abortController = new AbortController()
        },
        getAbortControllerSignal() {
            return this._abortController.signal
        },
        resetAbortController() {
            this._abortController = null;
        },
        toggleMiniCart() {
            console.log('(minicart.js) toggleMiniCart called');

            LiquifyHelper.handleTriggerClick();

            this.getCart();
        },

        /**
         * Get the cart data.
         */
        async getCart() {
            this.initAbortController()
            await fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart.js', {
                method: 'GET',
                signal: this.getAbortControllerSignal(),
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            })
                .then(response => response.json())
                .then(data => {
                    this.resetAbortController();

                    this.cart.item_count = data.item_count;

                    this.cart.items = data.items.map((item) => {
                        item.title = this.htmlspecialchars_decode(item.title)
                        return item
                    })


                    this.cart.total_price = data.total_price;
                    this.cart.total_weight = data.total_weight;
                    this.cart.total_discount = data.total_discount;
                    this.cart.original_total_price = data.original_total_price;

                    this.$dispatch('carttotalitems', data.item_count);
                    this.$dispatch('currentcartitems', this.cart.items);
                })
                .catch((error) => {
                    console.error('Error:', error);
                });
        },

        /**
         * @param key
         * @param quantity
         */
        increaseCartItemQuantity(key, quantity) {
            this.updateCartItemQuantity(key, parseInt(quantity) + 1);
        },

        /**
         * @param key
         * @param quantity
         */
        decreaseCartItemQuantity(key, quantity) {
            this.updateCartItemQuantity(key, parseInt(quantity) - 1);
        },

        /**
         * Update the cart item.
         *
         * @param key
         * @param quantity
         */
        updateCartItemQuantity(key, quantity) {
            this.initAbortController();
            console.log('updateCartItemQuantity(): key, quantity: ', key, quantity);
            this.cart.items.filter((product)  => {
                if(product.key === key) {
                    product.quantity = quantity
                }
            })
            let updates = {};
            updates[key] = quantity;
            fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart/update.js', {
                method: 'POST',
                signal: this.getAbortControllerSignal(),
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                body: JSON.stringify({ updates }),
            })
                .then(response => response.json())
                .then(data => {
                    this.resetAbortController();
                    console.log('updateCartItemQuantity(): ', data);

                    this.$dispatch('cartupdated', this.cart.items);
                    this.$dispatch('showcartmessage', { status: data.status, message: data.message, description: data.description });
                })
                .catch((error) => {
                    console.error('Error:', error);
                    this.$dispatch('showcartmessage', { status: error?.status, message: error, description: error });
                });
        },

        /**
         * Format monetary values.
         */
        moneyFormat(value, minor = true) {
            return LiquifyHelper.moneyFormat(value, minor)
        },

        htmlspecialchars_decode(string) {
            return LiquifyHelper.htmlspecialchars_decode(string)
        },

        /**
         * Shows the minicart api message
         * @param event
         */
        showCartMessage(event) {
            if(event?.detail?.status) {
                this.cart.response.result = event.detail ?? {}
                this.cart.response.show = true
                setTimeout(() => {
                    this.cart.response.result = {};
                    this.cart.response.show = false
                }, this.cart.response.timeout ?? 5000)
            }
        },

        async returnCartItems() {
            await this.getCart();

            this.$dispatch('currentcartitems', this.cart.items);
        },

        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set open(open) { // deprecated use  LiquifyHelper instead
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set open" in mini_cart')
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get open() { // deprecated use  LiquifyHelper instead
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get open" in mini_cart')
            return false;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set note(note) { // deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set note" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.note = note;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get note() { // deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get note" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart?.note;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set attributes(attributes) { // deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set attributes" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.attributes = attributes;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get attributes() { // deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get attributes" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart?.attributes;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set original_total_price(original_total_price) { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set original_total_price" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.original_total_price = original_total_price;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get original_total_price() { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get original_total_price" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart.original_total_price;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set total_price(total_price) { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set total_price" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.total_price = total_price;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get total_price() { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get total_price" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart.total_price;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set total_discount(total_discount) { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set total_discount" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.total_discount = total_discount;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get total_discount() { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get total_discount" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart.total_discount;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set total_weight(total_weight) { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set total_weight" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.total_discount = total_weight;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get total_weight() { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get total_weight" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart.total_weight;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set item_count(item_count) { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set item_count" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.total_discount = item_count;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get item_count() { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get item_count" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart.item_count;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set items_subtotal_price(items_subtotal_price) { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set items_subtotal_price" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.items_subtotal_price = items_subtotal_price;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get items_subtotal_price() { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get items_subtotal_price" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart.items_subtotal_price;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set products(products) {
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set products" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.items = products;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get products() {
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get products" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart.items;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set requires_shipping(requires_shipping) {
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set requires_shipping" in mini_cart')
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get requires_shipping() {
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get requires_shipping" in mini_cart')
            return false;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set currency(currency) { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set currency" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.currency = currency;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get currency() { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get currency" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart.currency;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set cart_level_discount_applications(cart_level_discount_applications) { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set cart_level_discount_applications" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.cart_level_discount_applications = cart_level_discount_applications;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get cart_level_discount_applications() { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get cart_level_discount_applications" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart.cart_level_discount_applications;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set cartApiResponse(cartApiResponse) { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set cartApiResponse" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.cartApiResponse = cartApiResponse;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get cartApiResponse() { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get cartApiResponse" in mini_cart')
            return this.cart.response;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        set total(total) { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "set total" in mini_cart')
            this.cart.item_count = total.items;
            this.cart.total_price = total.price;
            this.cart.total_weight = total.weight;
            this.cart.total_discount = total.discount;
        },
        /**
         * @deprecated
         */
        get total() { //deprecated
            //console.warn('Deprecated "get total" in mini_cart')
            return {
                items: this.cart.item_count,
                price: this.cart.total_price,
                weight: this.cart.total_weight,
                discount: this.cart.total_discount,
            };
        }
    }))


});

</script>
<div data-delay="0" data-hover="false" li-element="mini-cart" class="nav_dropdown-2 hidden w-dropdown" x-data="handleMinicart()" x-init="getCart()" @cartupdated.window="getCart()" @setcartitem.window="updateCartItemQuantity($event.detail.product, $event.detail.quantity)" @increasecartitem.window="increaseCartItemQuantity($event.detail.product, $event.detail.quantity)" @decreasecartitem.window="decreaseCartItemQuantity($event.detail.product, $event.detail.quantity)" @toggleminicart.window="toggleMiniCart()" @showcartmessage.window="showCartMessage" @getcartitems.window="returnCartItems">
                <div li-element="mini-cart-toggle" class="nav_shop-icon-3 w-dropdown-toggle" @click="$dispatch('cartupdated.window')">
                  <div li-element="mini-cart-item-count" class="nav_shop-quantity-3" x-data="{ items: 0 }" x-text="items" @carttotalitems.window="items = event.detail">0</div>

/* ---------- Zoek-modal ---------- */
html.search-open,
html.search-open body { overflow: hidden; }
html.search-open .search-modal { display: flex; }
.search-modal .search-modal_overlay { animation: sm-fade-in .25s ease both; }
.search-modal .search-modal_dialog { animation: sm-pop-in .4s cubic-bezier(.2, .9, .3, 1.15) both; }
.search-modal.is-closing .search-modal_overlay { animation: sm-fade-out .22s ease both; }
.search-modal.is-closing .search-modal_dialog { animation: sm-pop-out .22s ease both; }
@keyframes sm-fade-in { from { opacity: 0; } to { opacity: 1; } }
@keyframes sm-fade-out { from { opacity: 1; } to { opacity: 0; } }

  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(28px) scale(.96) rotate(-1deg); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: none; }
}

  from { opacity: 1; transform: none; }
  to { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(14px) scale(.98); }
}

}

/* Bestaand zoekformulier binnen de modal */

}

        <script>
(() => {
  function init() {
    const modal = document.querySelector('[data-search-modal]');
    if (!modal || modal.dataset.searchInit === 'true') return;
    modal.dataset.searchInit = 'true';
    // Los van de sticky header plaatsen, zodat de modal altijd boven alles ligt
    document.body.appendChild(modal);
    const root = document.documentElement;
    const dialog = modal.querySelector('[data-search-dialog]') || modal;
    const input = modal.querySelector('input[type="search"]');
    let lastFocus = null;
    let closeTimer = null;
    const isOpen = () => root.classList.contains('search-open');
    function open() {
      clearTimeout(closeTimer);
      lastFocus = document.activeElement;
      modal.classList.remove('is-closing');
      root.classList.add('search-open');
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (input) {
          input.focus({ preventScroll: true });
          if (input.value) input.select();
        }
      });
    }
    function close() {
      if (!isOpen() || modal.classList.contains('is-closing')) return;
      modal.classList.add('is-closing');
      closeTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        root.classList.remove('search-open');
        modal.classList.remove('is-closing');
        if (lastFocus && lastFocus.focus) lastFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      }, 220);
    }
    document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
      if (event.target.closest('[data-search-open]')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        open();
      } else if (event.target.closest('[data-search-close]')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        close();
      }
    });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape' && isOpen()) {
        close();
        return;
      }
      const control = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('[data-search-open], [data-search-close]');
      if (control && (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        control.hasAttribute('data-search-open') ? open() : close();
        return;
      }
      // Sneltoetsen: '/' of Cmd/Ctrl + K
      const typing = /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(event.target.tagName) || event.target.isContentEditable;
      if (!isOpen() && ((event.key === 'k' && (event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey)) || (event.key === '/' && !typing))) {
        event.preventDefault();
        open();
        return;
      }
      // Focus binnen de modal houden
      if (isOpen() && event.key === 'Tab') {
        const items = Array.from(dialog.querySelectorAll('a[href], button, input, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'))
          .filter((el) => el.offsetParent !== null);
        if (!items.length) return;
        const first = items[0];
        const last = items[items.length - 1];
        if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
          event.preventDefault();
          last.focus();
        } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
          event.preventDefault();
          first.focus();
        }
      }
    });
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
  } else {
    init();
  }
})();
</script>

            <div class="hidden w-embed w-script">
              <script>
(() => {
  const EXPANDED_MAX_HEIGHT = 420;
  const STORAGE_KEY = 'biersalon-filter-state';
  // Voorkom dat browser &eacute;n ons script tegelijk scroll proberen te herstellen
  if ('scrollRestoration' in history) {
    history.scrollRestoration = 'manual';
  }
  function normalizeText(value) {
    return value
      .toLowerCase()
      .normalize('NFD')
      .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
      .trim();
  }
  function getWrapperKey(wrapper, index) {
    const heading =
      wrapper.querySelector('.filter-heading')?.textContent?.trim();
    return heading || `filter-${index}`;
  }
  function getSavedState() {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(
        sessionStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || '{}'
      );
    } catch (error) {
      return {};
    }
  }
  function saveFilterState() {
    const wrappers = Array.from(
      document.querySelectorAll(
        '[data-filter-collapse="wrapper"]'
      )
    );
    const filters = {};
    wrappers.forEach((wrapper, index) => {
      const key = getWrapperKey(wrapper, index);
      const toggle = wrapper.querySelector(
        '[data-filter-collapse="toggle"]'
      );
      const search = wrapper.querySelector(
        '[data-filter-collapse="search"]'
      );
      filters[key] = {
        expanded:
          toggle?.dataset.expanded === 'true',
        search:
          search?.value || ''
      };
    });
    const state = {
      path: window.location.pathname,
      scrollY: window.scrollY,
      filters
    };
    sessionStorage.setItem(
      STORAGE_KEY,
      JSON.stringify(state)
    );
  }
  function initFilterCollapse(scope = document) {
    const savedState = getSavedState();
    scope
      .querySelectorAll('[data-filter-collapse="wrapper"]')
      .forEach((wrapper, index) => {
        if (
          wrapper.dataset.collapseInitialized === 'true'
        ) {
          return;
        }
        const list = wrapper.querySelector(
          '[data-filter-collapse="list"]'
        );
        const toggle = wrapper.querySelector(
          '[data-filter-collapse="toggle"]'
        );
        const toggleText = toggle?.querySelector(
          '[data-filter-collapse="toggle-text"]'
        );
        const searchWrapper = wrapper.querySelector(
          '[data-filter-collapse="search-wrapper"]'
        );
        const search = wrapper.querySelector(
          '[data-filter-collapse="search"]'
        );
        if (!list || !toggle) return;
        const collapsedLabel =
          toggle.dataset.labelCollapsed || 'Bekijk alle';
        const expandedLabel =
          toggle.dataset.labelExpanded || 'Minder';
        const items = Array.from(
          list.querySelectorAll('.filter-item')
        );
        if (items.length <= 5) {
          toggle.style.display = 'none';
          if (searchWrapper) {
            searchWrapper.hidden = true;
          }
          return;
        }
        wrapper.dataset.collapseInitialized = 'true';
        const key = getWrapperKey(wrapper, index);
        const savedFilterState =
          savedState.filters?.[key];
        function showAllItems() {
          items.forEach((item) => {
            item.style.display = '';
          });
        }
        function getCollapsedHeight() {
          const first = items[0];
          const fifth = items[4];
          if (!first || !fifth) return 0;
          const listRect =
            list.getBoundingClientRect();
          const fifthRect =
            fifth.getBoundingClientRect();
          return (
            fifthRect.top -
            listRect.top +
            fifthRect.height * 0.5
          );
        }
        function updateExpandedHeight() {
          requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            const height = Math.min(
              list.scrollHeight,
              EXPANDED_MAX_HEIGHT
            );
            list.style.maxHeight = `${height}px`;
          });
        }
        function filterItems() {
          if (!search) return;
          const query =
            normalizeText(search.value);
          items.forEach((item) => {
            const label =
              item.querySelector(
                '.filter_form_checkbox-label'
              )?.textContent ||
              item.textContent;
            const match =
              normalizeText(label).includes(query);
            item.style.display =
              match ? '' : 'none';
          });
          updateExpandedHeight();
        }
        function collapse(save = true) {
          if (search) {
            search.value = '';
          }
          if (searchWrapper) {
            searchWrapper.hidden = true;
          }
          showAllItems();
          list.style.overflowY = 'hidden';
          list.style.maxHeight =
            `${getCollapsedHeight()}px`;
          toggle.dataset.expanded = 'false';
          if (toggleText) {
            toggleText.textContent = collapsedLabel;
          }
          if (save) {
            saveFilterState();
          }
        }
        function expand(save = true) {
          if (searchWrapper) {
            searchWrapper.hidden = false;
          }
          list.style.overflowY = 'auto';
          toggle.dataset.expanded = 'true';
          if (toggleText) {
            toggleText.textContent = expandedLabel;
          }
          updateExpandedHeight();
          if (save) {
            saveFilterState();
          }
        }
        if (search) {
          search.addEventListener(
            'input',
            () => {
              filterItems();
              saveFilterState();
            }
          );
        }
        toggle.addEventListener(
          'click',
          (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            if (
              toggle.dataset.expanded === 'true'
            ) {
              collapse();
            } else {
              expand();
              if (search) {
                setTimeout(() => {
                  search.focus();
                }, 100);
              }
            }
          }
        );
        /*
         * Herstel vorige toestand.
         */
        if (
          savedState.path ===
            window.location.pathname &&
          savedFilterState?.expanded
        ) {
          expand(false);
          if (
            search &&
            savedFilterState.search
          ) {
            search.value =
              savedFilterState.search;
            filterItems();
          }
        } else {
          collapse(false);
        }
      });
  }
  /*
   * Vlak voordat een Shopify-filterlink wordt geopend:
   * scroll + open/dicht-status bewaren.
   */
  document.addEventListener(
    'click',
    (event) => {
      const filterLink = event.target.closest(
        '.filter_form_checkbox'
      );
      if (!filterLink) return;
      saveFilterState();
    },
    true
  );
  document.addEventListener(
    'DOMContentLoaded',
    () => {
      const savedState = getSavedState();
      initFilterCollapse();
      /*
       * Eerst de filters hun oude hoogte teruggeven,
       * daarna pas scrollpositie herstellen.
       */
      if (
        savedState.path ===
          window.location.pathname &&
        typeof savedState.scrollY === 'number'
      ) {
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            window.scrollTo({
              top: savedState.scrollY,
              left: 0,
              behavior: 'instant'
            });
          });
        });
      }
    }
  );
  /*
   * Voor eventueel opnieuw gerenderde filtermarkup.
   */
  const observer =
    new MutationObserver(() => {
      initFilterCollapse();
    });
  observer.observe(document.body, {
    childList: true,
    subtree: true
  });
})();
</script>
              <style>
[data-filter-collapse="list"] {
  transition: max-height 0.35s ease;
}
[data-filter-collapse="search-wrapper"][hidden] {
  display: none !important;
}
</style>
              <script>
(() => {
  const STORAGE_KEY = 'biersalon-filter-panel-collapsed';
  function initFilterPanels() {
    document
      .querySelectorAll('[data-filter-panel="panel"]')
      .forEach((panel) => {
        if (panel.dataset.filterPanelInitialized === 'true') return;
        const toggle = panel.querySelector(
          '[data-filter-panel="toggle"]'
        );
        const content = panel.querySelector(
          '[data-filter-panel="content"]'
        );
        if (!toggle || !content) return;
        panel.dataset.filterPanelInitialized = 'true';
        function setCollapsed(collapsed, save = true) {
          panel.dataset.collapsed = collapsed ? 'true' : 'false';
          toggle.setAttribute(
            'aria-expanded',
            collapsed ? 'false' : 'true'
          );
          if (save) {
            sessionStorage.setItem(
              STORAGE_KEY,
              collapsed ? 'true' : 'false'
            );
          }
        }
        toggle.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          const collapsed =
            panel.dataset.collapsed === 'true';
          setCollapsed(!collapsed);
        });
        const savedState =
          sessionStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        setCollapsed(savedState === 'true', false);
      });
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      initFilterPanels
    );
